                                                             Oven Fried Potatoes I
Ingredients                           Directions
5 pounds red potatoes, thinly        Preheat oven to 400 degrees F (200 degrees C). Grease a 9x13 inch
sliced                               casserole dish.
1 onion, finely chopped
6 slices bacon, chopped              Place potatoes in an even layer in greased dish. Sprinkle onion and
1/2 cup butter, diced                bacon over potatoes, then dot with butter. Cover dish with foil.
salt and pepper to taste
                                     Bake in preheated oven for 45 minutes. Remove foil and stir
                                     potatoes. Turn oven to broiler setting and allow potatoes to brown
                                     for approximately 5 minutes, until crispy. You may need to stir every
                                     minute or so to achieve even browning. Serve immediately.

                                               Southern Fried Chicken Livers
Ingredients                              Directions
1 pound chicken livers                  Place the chicken livers in a colander, and rinse with water. Drain
1 egg                                   the livers well. Whisk together the egg and milk in a shallow bowl
1/2 cup milk                            until well blended. Place the flour, garlic powder, and salt and
1 cup all-purpose flour                 pepper in a resealable plastic zipper bag, and shake to combine.
1 tablespoon garlic powder
salt and pepper to taste                Heat oil in a deep-fryer or large saucepan to 375 degrees F (190
1 quart vegetable oil for frying        degrees C).

                                        Place the chicken livers in the bowl of egg and milk mixture, and
                                        coat each liver. Place the livers, one at a time, into the plastic bag of
                                        flour mixture, and shake the bag to coat the each liver completely.

                                        Gently place the coated livers, a few at a time, into the hot oil.
                                        Cover the pan of oil with a frying screen to avoid getting burned by
                                        spatters of oil that will pop out as the livers fry. Deep fry the livers
                                        until crisp and golden brown, 5 to 6 minutes.

                                goat cheese on top of the tomato slices. Broil the polenta until the

                                 desired consistency, stirring constantly. For a thicker gravy add less
                                 milk; for a thinner gravy stir in more. Heat through, and season with
                                 salt and pepper to taste. Serve over chicken fried steak.
